However, autonomous UAV navigation in dense environments remains challenging under sparse perception and dynamic constraints. Most reinforcement learning (RL) methods lack explicit safety mechanisms, leading to unsafe exploration, unstable training, and risky behaviors, especially during high-speed flight. Even in safe RL approaches, safety is often enforced by projecting policy outputs onto a safe action set, which may introduce instability. Meanwhile, many learning-based methods rely on dense inputs or large networks, increasing computational burden and limiting lightweight onboard deployment. Facing the above challenges, we propose a safety-constrained perception-control integrated framework for UAV navigation. A lightweight network encodes sparse observations into collision-risk-aware features using asymmetric and depthwise separable convolutions. We formulate the task as a constrained Markov decision process within a hierarchical control architecture and solve it using a Lagrangian-based safe PPO algorithm. Curriculum learning further improves training stability. Experiments with varying obstacle densities and flight speeds demonstrate higher success rates, improved safety, and better efficiency than existing reinforcement learning baselines.

</details>


### [142] [VLA-Corrector: Lightweight Detect-and-Correct Inference for Adaptive Action Horizon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01804)
*Yi Pan,Miao Pan,Qi Lu,Jiaming Huang,Man Zhang,Siteng Huang,Xin Li,Jie Zhang,Yongliang Shen,Xuhong Zhang,Wenqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VLA-Corrector，一种轻量级矫正推理框架，通过潜在空间视觉监测器（LVM）在线检测视觉动态偏差并触发矫正重规划（OGG），实现事件触发的自适应动作视界，在不修改VLA骨干权重的前提下，打破动作分块策略的“预测-盲目执行”开环缺陷，提升长视距、密集接触机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作分块VLA策略采用固定视界的开环执行方式，在密集接触物理交互中，微小局部扰动会在开环盲区内迅速放大，导致累积误差和任务失败，缺乏闭环反应能力。

Method: 提出VLA-Corrector框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 潜在空间视觉监测器（LVM），在潜空间持续比较预测视觉特征与实际视觉特征的演化差异，在线检测视觉动态偏差；(2) 在线梯度引导（OGG），在检测到持续性偏差时触发截断事件，丢弃剩余过期动作并调用矫正重规划。该机制自然形成事件触发的自适应动作视界，无需修改骨干策略权重或重新训练。

Result: VLA-Corrector在不重新训练VLA骨干的前提下，能有效打断累积误差，保留动作分块的大部分效率优势，显著提升长视距、密集接触机器人操作任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VLA-Corrector通过检测-矫正机制缓解了静态动作视界下执行鲁棒性与策略调用频率之间的权衡，是一种可集成到不同VLA模型中的轻量级矫正推理框架。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently achieved strong progress in embodied intelligence. To reduce policy-call frequency while preserving temporal coherence, most generative policies adopt an action chunk mechanism, executing multiple future actions in an open-loop manner under a fixed action horizon. However, this "predict-then-blindly-execute" paradigm sacrifices closed-loop reactivity: in contact-rich physical interactions, even small local perturbations can rapidly amplify within the open-loop blind spot, leading to compounding errors and ultimately task failure. To address this limitation, we propose VLA-Corrector, a lightweight corrective inference framework for action-chunked VLA policies. Without modifying the backbone policy weights, VLA-Corrector introduces a lightweight Latent-space Vision Monitor (LVM) that continuously compares predicted and actual visual feature evolution, enabling online detection of visual dynamics deviations. Once persistent deviation is detected, the system triggers a truncation event, discards the remaining stale actions, and invokes corrective replanning via Online Gradient Guidance (OGG). The detect-and-correct mechanism of VLA-Corrector naturally induces an event-triggered adaptive action horizon: it preserves long-horizon execution when the current chunk remains reliable, and invokes short-horizon corrective replanning when execution begins to drift. In doing so, VLA-Corrector mitigates the trade-off imposed by static horizons between execution robustness and policy-call frequency. It can be integrated into different VLA models without further retraining the VLA backbone, interrupting compounding errors while preserving much of the efficiency benefit of action chunking and substantially improving robustness in long-horizon, contact-rich robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [143] [DL-SLAM: Enabling High-Fidelity Gaussian Splatting SLAM in Dynamic Environments based on Dual-Level Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01860)
*Ziheng Xu,Qingfeng Li,Xuefeng Liu,Chen Chen,Jianwei Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DL-SLAM提出了一种双层次概率框架，通过结合语义和几何信息生成动态概率图，在像素级和物体级分别计算动态概率，从而实现对动态高斯体的分类剔除，生成无伪影的静态地图，同时提升跟踪精度高达13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态SLAM方法要么直接丢弃预定义动态物体，忽略瞬态静态物体对位姿估计的几何约束价值；要么通过逐像素不确定度图量化运动幅度，但会将瞬态静态物体错误地集成到静态地图中导致持久伪影，且纯几何信息导致不确定度图的物体边界模糊。

Method: 提出双层次概率框架：1）结合语义和几何信息计算像素级动态概率图；2）将像素级概率提升到3D并聚合，推导每个实例的物体级动态概率；3）利用物体级概率对动态高斯体进行分类剔除，生成无伪影静态地图；4）静态地图提供几何一致性指导，反向优化像素级概率。

Result: DL-SLAM在跟踪精度上比现有方法提升最高13%，同时生成高保真语义地图。

Conclusion: 双层次概率框架有效解决了动态物体错误融入静态地图导致伪影的问题，通过语义与几何信息的融合以及概率的双向优化，显著提升了动态SLAM的鲁棒性和地图质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled significant progress in dense dynamic Simultaneous Localization And Mapping (SLAM). Prevailing methods typically discard predefined dynamic objects, ignoring that transiently static objects offer valuable geometric constraints for pose estimation. A recent work attempts to leverage this potential by employing per-pixel uncertainty maps to quantify the magnitude of motion. While this approach enables transiently static objects to enhance pose estimation, it erroneously integrates these objects into the static map, resulting in persistent artifacts. Moreover, its reliance on purely geometric information leads to ambiguous object boundaries in the uncertainty maps. To overcome these limitations, we present DL-SLAM, a monocular Gaussian Splatting SLAM system built upon a novel dual-level probabilistic framework. Our method computes dynamic probability maps by combining semantic and geometric information. These pixel-level probabilities are lifted to 3D and aggregated to derive an object-level dynamic probability for each instance. Object-level probability enables the categorical pruning of dynamic Gaussians, resulting in an artifact-free static map. The static map, in turn, provides a geometrically consistent guidance to refine the pixel-wise probabilities, enhancing their reliability. Experimental results demonstrate that DL-SLAM outperforms existing approaches, improving tracking accuracy by up to 13\% while generating high-fidelity semantic maps.

</details>


### [144] [SPLC: Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01925)
*Zixuan Chen,Hao Fu,Haiwen Hu,Shiquan Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SPLC算法，通过社交偏好反馈机制自动生成偏好数据，消除了离线强化学习中奖励函数设计的难题，在人群机器人导航任务中显著提升了社交合规行为，并在仿真和真实实验中均优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习在人群机器人导航中潜力巨大，但行人运动的固有复杂性使得设计有效的奖励函数以促进社交合规机器人行为始终是一个难题。

Method: 提出社交偏好学习算法(SPLC)，核心创新是引入社交偏好反馈机制，通过原则性的偏好评估标准自动生成偏好数据，显式建模行人动力学复杂性，缓解奖励偏差，系统量化广泛的社会规范。

Result: 将SPLC与离线RL方法集成后，在标准性能指标上持续优于最先进基线；TurtleBot4真实世界实验进一步验证了其在人机共存场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: SPLC通过自动化的社交偏好反馈机制有效消除了对精细奖励设计的需求，显著提升了机器人导航的社交合规性，在仿真和真实环境中均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) holds significant potential for crowd robot navigation in human-robot coexistence applications. However, the inherent complexity of pedestrian motion renders the design of effective reward functions for promoting socially compliant robot behaviors a persistent challenge. This paper proposes a Social Preference Learning for Crowd Robot Navigation (SPLC) algorithm to eliminate the need for detailed reward design. Its core innovation lies in the introduction of a social preference feedback mechanism to automatically generate preference data through principled preference evaluation criteria. By explicitly accounting for the intricacies of pedestrian dynamics, the pipeline mitigates the reward bias and facilitates the systematic quantification of broad social norms, thereby fostering socially compliant behaviors. Extensive experiments integrating SPLC with offline RL methods demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across standard performance metrics. Furthermore, real-world experiments on the TurtleBot4 further validate the effectiveness of SPLC in practical human-robot coexistence settings. Our code and video demos are available at https://github.com/sklus949/SPLC.

</details>


### [145] [PhysMani: Physics-principled 3D World Model for Dynamic Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01938)
*Peng Yun,Shouwang Huang,Hao Li,Jinxi Li,Jianan Wang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysMani提出了一种结合物理约束的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知动作策略模型的框架，用于在非结构化3D环境中操控快速动态目标，在16个任务的动态操控基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型在处理非结构化3D环境中快速移动的动态目标时，缺乏准确的3D几何建模和物理上有意义的预测能力。

Method: 提出PhysMani框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 物理约束的3D高斯世界模型，通过在线优化学习无散度高斯速度场，实现快速且物理可信的未来动态预测；(2) 未来感知动作策略模型，通过可学习token的交叉注意力模块整合预测的3D场景未来动态。

Result: 构建了包含16个任务的动态操控基准PhysMani-Bench，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均取得了优于强基线的成功率。

Conclusion: 物理约束的3D高斯世界模型与未来感知策略相结合，能够有效提升在非结构化环境中对快速动态目标的操控能力。

Abstract: Manipulating fast and dynamically moving targets in unstructured 3D environments remains challenging for embodied AI. Existing visual-language-action models and world models struggle with accurate 3D geometry and physically meaningful forecasting. We propose PhysMani, a framework that couples a physics-principled 3D Gaussian world model with a future-aware action policy model. The world model learns a divergence-free Gaussian velocity field via online optimization for fast and physically grounded future dynamics prediction. The policy model integrates the predicted 3D scene future dynamics through a learnable token based cross-attention module. We introduce PhysMani-Bench, a dynamic manipulation benchmark with 16 tasks, and demonstrate a superior success rate over strong baselines in both simulation and real-world robot experiments.

</details>


### [146] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCD SLAM是一种动态双目视觉SLAM框架，通过引入交叉视差概念检测动态特征点，并结合3D目标检测与卡尔曼滤波跟踪，在动态场景下显著提升轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉SLAM系统在动态环境中因静态世界假设而导致位姿估计和建图失败，需要一种能够同时处理场景中动态物体和动态特征的方法。

Method: 提出基于视差与交叉视差（Cross Disparity）差异的几何方法检测动态特征点（利用时间和立体不一致性），并集成SMOKE的3D目标检测模块与卡尔曼滤波跟踪进行物体级运动分类，实现静态与动态场景元素的鲁棒分离。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry和KITTI Raw数据集上，OCD SLAM相比ORB-SLAM2及多种最新动态SLAM方法在轨迹精度上取得显著提升；消融实验验证了交叉视差模块的有效性，能检测到被3D目标检测遗漏的动态特征。

Conclusion: 提出了一种特征级与物体级相结合的动态SLAM方案，交叉视差模块能够有效补充纯目标检测方法的不足，提升动态场景下的位姿估计鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents OCD SLAM, a dynamic stereo visual SLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM2 by jointly addressing dynamic objects and dynamic features in the scene. Usual visual SLAM systems operating in dynamic environments often fail in the presence of moving objects, due to the static-world assumption used in pose estimation and mapping. To address this predicament, we introduce a novel geometric approach based on the discrepancy between disparity and a newly proposed notion called ``cross disparity'', which exploits both temporal and stereo inconsistency to identify dynamic feature points. Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [147] [Cross-Platform Control for Autonomous Surface Vehicles via Adaptive Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02037)
*Ruiheng Jiang,Thomas Bi,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应强化学习方法，通过教师-学生架构推断平台动力学潜表征，实现单策略零样本跨平台轨迹跟踪，在两种真实水面艇上无需微调即达到接近专用控制器的精度，比非自适应基线提升最高58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人水面艇的水动力和驱动特性差异大，现有控制器大多针对单一平台设计，缺乏能零样本部署到不同平台的通用策略。

Method: 采用部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程框架，通过教师-学生架构学习潜表征来推断未知的平台动力学；在仿真中随机化艇体动力学参数训练策略，然后零样本部署到真实平台。

Result: 在两种不同真实平台上的实验中，自适应策略的位置平均绝对误差比非自适应基线降低最高58%，跟踪精度接近平台专用的调参控制器。

Conclusion: 仅依赖简单解析动力学模型而非高保真水动力仿真器，通过自适应强化学习即可实现有效的跨平台零样本迁移，显著优于非自适应基线。

Abstract: Autonomous surface vehicles vary widely in hydrodynamic and actuation characteristics, yet most controllers are designed for single-platform deployment. We present an adaptive reinforcement learning approach for trajectory tracking that enables zero-shot cross-platform deployment using a single policy. Since the deployment platform's dynamics are unknown to the policy, we address cross-platform generalization with the standard partial-observability approach of conditioning on interaction history, employing a teacher-student architecture in which a learned module infers a latent representation of the platform dynamics. The policy is trained in simulation under randomized vessel dynamics and is deployed zero-shot to two real-world platforms without any fine-tuning, despite relying on a simple analytical dynamics model rather than a high-fidelity hydrodynamic simulator. In real-world experiments on two different platforms, the adaptive policy outperforms non-adaptive learning-based baselines by up to 58% in position mean absolute error while approaching the tracking accuracy of a platform-specific tuned controller.

</details>


### [148] [Guided Action Flow: Q-Guided Inference for Flow-Matching Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02092)
*Liuhaichen Yang,Zhuang Jiang,Chenchao Sheng,Zezhi Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Guided Action Flow 提出了一种推理时引导框架，通过冻结预训练的流匹配视觉-语言-动作策略（SmolVLA）并训练一个动作块评论器来引导逆向流采样，在LIBERO任务上将单任务成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%，验证了无需重训练即可实现推理时引导的可行性，同时指出评论器泛化与不确定性感知引导是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配VLA策略通过迭代传输过程生成机器人动作块，这为测试时引导提供了机会，但现有方法需要重训练基础策略。作者希望在不重新训练基础策略的前提下，利用推理时引导来提升策略性能。

Method: 保持预训练SmolVLA策略冻结，训练一个动作块评论器（critic），该评论器从真实成功与失败 rollout 中学习，可条件于冻结的SmolVLA语言路径的任务描述特征，在采样过程中仅通过动作梯度进行引导。

Result: 在LIBERO任务上，单任务评论器将成功率从68.0%提升至82.0%（一个种子窗口）及从82.0%提升至86.0%（另一个种子窗口）；多家族任务描述评论器将验证成功率从46.0%提升至56.0%，但锁定保留测试集提升较小（65.0%到67.5%）。

Conclusion: Q引导推理对于冻结的流匹配VLA策略是可行的，但评论器的泛化能力与不确定性感知引导仍是核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action policies generate robot action chunks through an iterative transport process, creating an opportunity for test-time guidance without retraining the base policy. We study this opportunity in Guided Action Flow, an inference-time framework that keeps a pretrained SmolVLA policy frozen and uses a learned action-chunk critic to guide its reverse-time flow sampler. The critic is trained from real success and failure rollouts, can condition on task-description features from the frozen SmolVLA language pathway, and is used only through action gradients during sampling. We evaluate the approach on LIBERO manipulation tasks. A single-task critic improves success from 68.0% to 82.0% on one seed window and from 82.0% to 86.0% on another. A multi-family task-description critic improves validation success from 46.0% to 56.0%, while the locked held-out test gain is positive but modest, from 65.0% to 67.5%. These results support the feasibility of Q-guided inference for frozen flow-matching VLA policies, while showing that critic generalization and uncertainty-aware guidance remain the central bottlenecks.

</details>


### [149] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Way of Water框架，通过浏览器端的Studio工具（类似DAW）结合序列凸规划与模型预测控制，实现非编程艺术家对水面机器人编队的音乐响应式3D编舞，并在苏黎世湖（18艇）和威尼斯双年展（8艇）完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放水域的非线性流体动力学给机器人编队带来环境干扰和系统非线性的巨大挑战，现有方案局限于2D水面像素范式，缺乏面向非编程艺术家的高性能水上机器人编舞平台。

Method: 提出Way of Water Studio浏览器端时间线合成范式，将编队视为DAW乐器；集成序列凸规划（SCP）进行轨迹生成与模型预测控制（MPC）进行扰动抑制；构建完整信息物理系统：全向底盘、状态估计与控制栈、LTE/MQTT舰队链路及RTK-GPS时间同步。

Result: 在苏黎世湖18艇《天鹅湖》和威尼斯Forte Marghera 8艇《Time Space Existence 2025》两个部署中完成系统验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了流体机器人群体设计与部署的基础参考，将水上机器人表现力从2D水面扩展到3D体积域，为非编程艺术家提供了高性能编舞工具。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [150] [Bridge-WA: Predicting Where and How the World Changes for Robotic Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02195)
*Yongjie Bai,Hanting Wang,Mingtong Dai,Qijun Zhong,Yang Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bridge-WA 是一种轻量级世界-动作框架，通过从冻结的未来变化教师模型中蒸馏出三个紧凑先验（未来令牌、变化图、运动流图），使动作生成聚焦于场景变化，从而在无需密集未来图像生成的情况下提升操作任务的泛化能力和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型依赖大规模生成式世界模型或密集未来 rollout，计算成本高且将容量浪费在与控制弱相关的视觉细节上。本文旨在设计一种轻量级方案，仅捕捉动作相关的场景变化，避免冗余视觉重建。

Method: 从冻结的未来变化教师模型中蒸馏出三个紧凑先验：未来令牌（预期结果）、变化图（干预支持）和运动流图（局部过渡方向）。通过 WorldBridge 模块，利用多源注意力记忆和时空偏置将这些先验条件化到动作 Transformer 中，推理时移除教师模型。

Result: 在 VLABench、RoboTwin2.0、LIBERO-Plus 及真实机器人评估中，Bridge-WA 在任务成功率、进度和鲁棒性上均有提升，尤其在分布外视觉偏移下增益显著。

Conclusion: 通过将动作生成聚焦于场景变化的"何处"与"如何"，Bridge-WA 抑制了背景、光照、干扰物等无关外观因素，实现了更好的泛化，且无需部署时的密集未来图像生成。

Abstract: General-purpose vision-language-action models benefit from large vision-language priors, but effective manipulation also requires anticipating action-relevant scene changes. Existing world-action models often rely on large generative world models or dense future rollouts, which are expensive and spend capacity on visual details weakly coupled to control. We present Bridge-WA, a lightweight world-action framework that distills a frozen future-change teacher into three compact priors: future tokens for intended outcomes, change maps for intervention support, and motion-flow maps for local transition direction. A WorldBridge conditions the action transformer on these priors through multi-source attention memories and spatial-temporal biases, while the teacher model is removed at inference. Across VLABench, RoboTwin2.0, LIBERO-Plus and real-robot evaluations, Bridge-WA improves task success, progress, and robustness, with particularly clear gains under out-of-distribution visual shifts. By focusing action generation on where and how the scene will change, Bridge-WA suppresses nuisance appearance factors such as background, lighting, and distractors, leading to better generalization without deployment-time dense future-image generation. Code and visualizations are available at: https://hcplab-sysu.github.io/BRIDGE-WA .

</details>


### [151] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“执行器现实塑造”（Actuator Reality Shaping）的新范式，通过修改物理执行器的闭环行为以匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考模型，而非修改仿真器来匹配现实，从而实现强化学习策略从仿真到真实世界的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的Sim-to-real迁移常因理想执行器动力学与非线性、硬件相关的物理电机行为之间的差异而受限。传统方法通过增加仿真器保真度（如系统辨识、域随机化、学习执行器模型）来弥合差距，但这些方法复杂度高且难以泛化。

Method: 为每个关节配备一个二自由度前馈-反馈控制器，将参考响应整形与鲁棒稳定性解耦。通过整形物理执行器的闭环行为，使其匹配仿真中使用的理想化二阶参考动力学，从而为强化学习策略提供标准化的执行器接口。

Result: 在单关节高减速比伺服电机（带外部负载）和7自由度机械臂到达任务中，与标准伺服控制和代表性实到仿到实基线相比，执行器现实塑造显著降低了Sim-to-real跟踪误差，提升了零样本任务性能。并在轮腿机器人爬坡和人形机器人行走中验证了零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: 执行器现实塑造可作为跨不同硬件平台的机器人学习的可复用接口，实现无需任务级微调或学习执行器模型的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [152] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoFL-S低层视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测语言条件流场生成连续轨迹，在连续时间Habitat基准和真实场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言导航中高层指令推理、记忆等被广泛研究，但低层动作表示相对未被充分探索。

Method: CoFL-S框架预测语言条件流场，将VLN-CE整段指令转换为帧级局部监督，并引入连续时间Habitat基准进行低层动作接口评估。

Result: 在连续时间Habitat基准中，CoFL-S在不同规划频率下均优于动作token和动作块基线，零样本真实世界部署同样展现出优势。

Conclusion: 预测语言条件流场的低层动作表示方法在连续导航中有效，且能泛化到真实场景。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [153] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEUROSYMLAND是一个神经符号着陆点评估系统，通过概率语义场景图与符号约束推理，在72个模拟场景中实现61次成功评估（基线37-57次），并在100次硬件在环实验中展示了边缘设备上的可行性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯视觉学习方法在非结构化环境中易受地形变化影响且安全决策透明度不足，亟需一种在边缘无人机上可行、兼具鲁棒性与可解释性的着陆点评估方案。

Method: 将轻量感知模块与显式符号推理结合：从机载视觉输入构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），再利用符号规则约束评估地形平坦度、障碍物间距和空间一致性，实现感知不确定性下的结构化推理。

Result: 在72个多样地形模拟场景中完成61次成功评估，优于四个基线（37-57次）；100次硬件在环试验显示符号推理仅占端到端延迟极小部分，主要计算开销来自感知和PSSG构建。

Conclusion: NEUROSYMLAND在边缘约束无人机硬件上实现了着陆点评估栈的可行部署，兼具鲁棒性、可解释性和有限的资源占用，代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [154] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DJI Tello无人机的集成智能系统，通过模块化架构集成面部检测、面部识别和单目深度估计三种AI功能，利用轻量级神经网络在嵌入式硬件上实现实时处理，并通过Web界面进行控制。系统在人员跟踪、室内扫描和自主线跟踪等任务中表现出色，验证了在低成本硬件上部署先进AI技术的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业无人机解决方案成本高、封闭性强，缺乏对低成本、开源、易用型智能无人机系统的研究。作者希望开发一套经济实惠、基于开源技术的个人助理无人机系统，使AI驱动的自主飞行能力更加普及。

Method: 采用DJI Tello作为飞行平台，设计模块化架构集成三个核心AI功能：人脸检测、人脸识别和单目深度估计。通过Web界面实现无人机控制和实时视频监控，Python后端服务器处理视觉数据并使用轻量级神经网络模型进行推理，优化以适配嵌入式系统资源限制。

Result: 系统在真实场景下表现出稳健性能，成功实现了人员跟踪、室内扫描、基于虚拟传感器的自主线跟踪等任务，验证了轻量级AI模型在受限硬件上的实时运行能力。

Conclusion: 本项目验证了在低成本、资源受限的无人机平台上部署先进AI技术的可行性，为未来自主无人机在军事、救援和监控等领域的进一步研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [155] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合数据策略（连续相机运动+多样化静态视角），通过减少虚假相关性来增强VLA模型的空间泛化能力，实验表明该策略优于单纯增加静态视角的方法，且适用于多种模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在空间泛化方面表现脆弱，单纯增加视角数量不足以解决问题，模型容易陷入捷径学习（Shortcut Learning），依赖虚假相关性而非真正的空间关系。

Method: 使用双臂设置，一臂执行操作任务，另一臂作为移动环境相机。系统评估三种数据分布模式：固定视角、多固定视角和移动视角。提出混合策略：连续相机运动与多样化静态视角结合。

Result: 混合策略显著优于单纯增加静态视角的方法；能有效减少虚假相关性，使VLA模型泛化到未见过的相机位姿和物体配置；捷径学习易感性和空间泛化困难是跨不同架构的普遍特性。

Conclusion: 所有评估模型（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均能从混合数据策略中获益；该策略在维持训练稳定性的同时大幅提升空间泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [156] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEFT是一个面向全尺寸重型负重人形机器人的遥操作框架，通过特权运动引导（PMG）和窗口化负重课程（WPC）解决了大惯性、紧平衡裕度带来的噪声敏感问题，在L7人形机器人上实现了高达24kg负重下的运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用运动跟踪和遥操作框架大多在紧凑平台上验证，未涉及真实负重交互，全尺寸人形机器人在大惯性和紧密平衡裕度下对噪声、漂移和重定向误差极为敏感，同时其负重潜力未被充分利用。

Method: 提出HEFT框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG) - 从可部署的含噪VR参考中学习，通过物理上合理的重建参考进行引导；2) Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) - 结合专家引导的负重上限，逐步学习鲁棒的重负重跟踪能力。

Result: 在L7（175cm, 65kg）全尺寸人形机器人上部署，实现了包括转弯、前/后行走、深蹲等运动在高达24kg负重下的跟踪。

Conclusion: HEFT有效解决了全尺寸人形机器人大负重遥操作中的关键挑战，验证了利用廉价VR设备实现鲁棒重负重运动跟踪的可行性。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [157] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACID框架，通过循环动作一致性（预测轨迹的逆向动力学验证）改进基于世界模型的决策时规划，在多种模型和任务中提升效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准决策时规划仅评估预测终端状态与目标的接近程度，忽略了中间过渡的可实现性，导致预测轨迹看似合理但实际环境 rollout 会偏离。

Method: 提出ACID框架，引入循环动作一致性：通过逆向动力学模型从预测过渡中反向推断动作，应与原始条件动作一致；将该逐步残差通过尺度不变自适应权重融入规划成本。

Result: 在四种动作条件世界模型和六项任务（刚体/可变形操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID持续改进规划质量，并以更少的规划计算量达到与基线相当的精度。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性是一种有效且高效的规划正则化手段，能够在不增加计算负担的情况下提升基于世界模型的决策时规划的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [158] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIME，一个语言条件相机运动生成模型，能够根据当前RGB观测和自由形式的自然语言意图，预测下一观测的相对目标相机位姿。该模型从自我中心视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头，将被动的人类视频转化为意图驱动的主动感知监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人常需移动相机才能执行任务（如检查物体、揭示遮挡区域），但语言条件相机运动作为一类动作尚未被充分探索。现有工作关注视觉-语言导航和操作策略，缺乏对语言驱动的相机运动生成的研究。

Method: 从自我中心视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督，将合理的意图和观测增益描述与相对SE(3)目标位姿配对。提出LIME模型，结合自回归观测增益输出与连续流匹配位姿头，联合预测下一视图应揭示的内容并表示多假设目标视图。

Result: 实验表明LIME能够从被动的人类视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，将普通自我中心视频转化为意图感知主动感知的监督信号，并在下游机器人任务中表现良好。

Conclusion: LIME证明了语言条件相机运动生成作为一种新型任务的可行性，通过从被动视频中学习主动感知行为，为机器人视觉感知提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [159] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample是一种用于真实机器人强化学习的数据增强框架，通过物理 rollout、世界模型生成和策略改进之间的真实-合成闭环，结合策略节奏学习（PPL）进行样本选择与调度，在减少59%训练步骤的同时将策略成功率提升28%，并将世界模型视觉保真度提升19.4dB PSNR和0.47 SSIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人强化学习面临交互成本高的问题，每次物理 rollout 代价昂贵且仅反映单一动作-结果路径；而模仿学习受限于演示覆盖面。现有方法难以在低成本下获得高效策略。

Method: 提出WorldSample框架，构建物理rollout与世界模型生成之间的真实-合成闭环：在真实rollout基础上通过后训练的世界模型生成高保真合成转移数据；引入策略节奏学习（PPL）进行样本选择与调度，平衡有用增强与值估计过拟合，减轻幻觉噪声。

Result: 在涉及接触丰富和精密操作的机器人操作任务中，WorldSample相比基线将策略成功率提升28%，训练步骤减少59%；世界模型视觉保真度在PSNR上提升19.4dB，SSIM提升0.47。

Conclusion: 真实-合成闭环能有效同时提升策略性能和世界模型质量，PPL机制是平衡增强数据利用与抑制误差放大的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [160] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务无关预训练（TAP）框架，将VLA模型的学习解耦为物理能力（如何移动）和语义对齐（做什么）两个目标，利用廉价无标签交互数据（如离轨轨迹和机器人自主探索）通过自监督逆动力学目标预训练运动先验，再用少量专家数据将先验与语言对齐。在SIMPLER基准上，TAP与使用超100万条专家轨迹训练的模型性能相当，标注数据量却少几个数量级；在真实WidowX平台上，TAP在相机扰动下仍保持25%成功率，而大规模基线降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于专家演示数据的稀缺性，因为收集大规模包含观测、指令和动作的三元组成本极高。现有方法将物理能力和语义对齐两个学习目标混为一谈，导致对语言标注数据的过度依赖。

Method: 提出分解假说（Decomposition Hypothesis），将学习目标解耦为物理能力获取和语义对齐两个独立目标。两阶段框架TAP：第一阶段使用自监督逆动力学目标从廉价无标签数据（离轨轨迹和机器人自主探索）中学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段用极少量专家数据将这些先验与语言指令对齐。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP与使用超100万条专家轨迹训练的模型性能相当，标注数据量少几个数量级，相比标准行为克隆有10%绝对提升。在真实WidowX平台上，TAP在相机扰动下保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线（如RT-2等）完全失效。

Conclusion: 任务无关预训练能够产生鲁棒且可迁移的物理表征，为具身智能提供了一条可扩展的发展路径，减少了对大规模标注专家数据的依赖。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [161] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态来学习拦截入侵者控制策略的方法，通过可微四旋翼动力学的分析策略梯度方法，实现了高达10 m/s的敏捷拦截，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法假设可获取相对位置或距离信息，但这在仅使用被动单目相机传感器的实际应用中难以实现，因此需要一种仅依赖方向信息即可实现拦截的策略学习方法。

Method: 利用可微四旋翼动力学模型，通过分析策略梯度方法学习控制策略，仅使用三维方向单位向量和拦截器自身状态作为输入。

Result: 所提方法在高达10 m/s速度下实现敏捷拦截，相比使用简化质点动力学的基线方法平均性能提升30%。

Conclusion: 仅依赖方向信息的分析策略梯度方法能够有效学习高速拦截策略，克服了实际应用中位置信息不可获取的限制。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [162] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，通过万向节将圆柱体主体安装在四旋翼上方形成飞行的倒立摆系统。采用简化姿态表示和自适应反步控制实现轨迹跟踪，并通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为，仿真和实验验证了该平台作为推力矢量控制测试床的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为推力矢量控制策略提供低成本、低风险的验证平台，解决传统火箭测试成本高、风险大的问题。

Method: 将耦合系统建模为单轴对称刚体，采用二维球面上的简化姿态表示显式利用轴向对称性并解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；设计自适应反步控制器实现存在未知常值扰动下的几乎全局轨迹跟踪；采用控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；将四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量。

Result: 仿真和室内运动捕捉实验均验证了该平台能够实现精确的轨迹跟踪和有效的扰动补偿，确认了QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制机器人车辆通用测试平台的适用性。

Conclusion: QuadRocket为推力矢量控制研究提供了一个低成本、低风险且有效的实验验证平台，其控制架构具有良好的鲁棒性和跟踪性能。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [163] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出可控神经变分智能体（CNeVA）框架，通过闭合形式共轭变分更新从每通道折扣回报中推断高斯行为潜变量，并利用混合通道掩码课程训练的条件化整流流轨迹生成器实现可控交通仿真，在Waymo数据集上取得竞争性真实感的同时提供每通道可控制性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实交通仿真需要既能模仿记录行为又能沿可解释轴进行引导的智能体，以实现变量隔离、特定边缘案例复现及无真实世界风险的自动驾驶系统测试，而现有高排名模仿模型缺乏每通道可控制性。

Method: 1) 每通道折扣回报的闭合形式共轭变分更新推断高斯行为潜变量；2) 基于混合通道掩码课程训练的整流流轨迹生成器，实现无分类器引导；3) 软资格门控机制（soft eligibility gates）用平滑指数衰减替代硬二进制阈值，保留近阈值智能体的梯度信号。

Result: 1) 在Waymo数据集上达到竞争性真实感；2) 速度和加速度引导产生单调响应，无失速导致的奖励破解；3) 引入软资格门控后安全可控性显著且单调；4) 在上下文残差回报度量下实现可引导地图合规性。

Conclusion: 可控制性指标必须与物理合理性护栏一同解读，以避免奖励破解混淆；CNeVA在保持仿真真实感的同时提供了现有方法缺乏的每通道精细可控能力。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [164] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Embodied.cpp，一个面向具身AI模型的便携式C++推理运行时，通过五层架构设计实现多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展接口，在VLA模型和WAM上验证了部署效率与高精度保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理运行时主要面向请求-响应服务模式，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制内的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先批量1推理、以及超越固定令牌I/O的可扩展具身接口。

Method: 通过对代表性VLA模型和WAM的架构分析，提取共享执行路径并组织为五层：输入适配器、序列构建器、骨干执行、头插件和部署适配器。提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理、可扩展算子和I/O支持，通过单一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人和模拟器的部署。

Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上分别达到100.0%和91.0%的闭环任务成功率；在WAM基准测试中将LingBot-VA Transformer块的显存从312.2 MiB降至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时显著提升了部署效率，能够支持多种具身模型架构，有效弥合了具身AI模型与实际部署之间的差距。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [165] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VT-WAM，一种视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，在接触丰富操作任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要策略对局部形变、压力、滑移和摩擦力做出反应，但这些触觉线索在时间上稀疏且在视觉观测中常不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测输入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉形变动力学。

Method: VT-WAM包含两个关键设计：(1) 非对称混合Transformer (MoT) 注意力机制，桥接首帧视觉锚点与时序触觉动力学；(2) 接触门控的动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导 (AVTAG)，在接触阶段鼓励动作查询依赖触觉证据。所有预测在统一的流匹配框架中联合学习。

Result: 在6项真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率达71.67%，相比Fast-WAM提升26.67%，相比OmniVTLA提升35.84%。消融实验表明建模触觉形变动力学和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触丰富任务均至关重要。

Conclusion: VT-WAM证明了在统一框架中联合学习视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测的有效性，为接触丰富操作任务中触觉动力学的建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Quantum-Inspired Vision: Leveraging Wave-Particle Duality for Low-Illumination Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01731)
*Yiquan Gao*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究通过将图像建模为概率波函数而非确定性状态，对数据相对论不确定性（DRU）框架进行了理论扩展，建立了从物理到AI的图像增强范式，并提供了可解释AI方法来阐明DRU如何减轻照明偏差并保持对数据噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRU框架在解释如何利用光的固有物理不确定性进行图像增强方面缺乏系统的理论讨论，需要形式化的理论扩展来建立物理到AI的范式。

Method: 将图像建模为概率波函数，整合波粒二象性原理，形式化描述DRU系统流程，并构建可解释AI框架。

Result: 提出了一个从物理到AI的理论范式，为DRU框架提供了严格的可解释AI方法，能够解释其如何减轻照明偏差并保持对数据噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该范式增强了对DRU框架的理论理解，为图像增强提供了基于物理不确定性的可解释性理论基础。

Abstract: This study provides a theoretical expansion of the recent Data Relativistic Uncertainty (DRU) framework by formalizing a physics-to-AI paradigm for image enhancement. By modeling images as probabilistic wave functions rather than deterministic states, the paradigm explicitly integrates wave-particle duality to illustrate the system flow of how DRU leverages the intrinsic physical uncertainty of light, a dimension requiring further theoretical discussion. Consequently, this paradigm provides a rigorous Explainable AI (XAI) approach that enhances the interpretability of how DRU mitigates illumination bias and maintains robustness against data noise.

</details>


### [167] [Population-Scale Segmentation of Penile Tissue in DIXON MRI using Deep Learning for Quantitative Phenotyping in Male Reproductive Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02127)
*Jan Ernsting,Gunnar Paul Kordes,Nils Johannaber,Lynn Ogoniak,Wolfgang Roll,Tim Hahn,Alexander Siegfried Busch,Benjamin Risse*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的全阴茎MRI分割框架，使用3D nnU-Net架构在DIXON MRI上实现了全自动阴茎分割，在34,412名UK Biobank参与者中验证了其可重复性和群体可扩展性，为男性生殖健康的大规模影像学研究提供了技术资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阴茎测量依赖外部长度或周长，难以标准化且无法捕捉内部结构；MRI可进行体内全阴茎体积评估，但缺乏自动化分割方法。因此需要建立一种可群体规模部署的自动化全器官体积测量方法，以支持男性生殖疾病的多组学和临床研究。

Method: 使用专家标注训练集（145名受试者，13,050张标注切片）和双标注独立测试基准（24名受试者，2,160张双标注切片），优化3D nnU-Net架构进行多通道DIXON MRI全阴茎分割，并在UK Biobank的34,412名参与者中部署。

Result: 5折交叉验证Dice得分为0.90；独立测试集Dice为0.92，Hausdorff距离为3.58；在2,282名男性中的纵向评估显示高重测信度（r=0.87）；成功在34,412名参与者中实现自动化定量。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了一种可重复、群体可扩展的MRI阴茎解剖评估方法，能够量化包括外部和内部成分在内的全阴茎组织，并将公开训练模型权重，为泌尿影像学和男性生殖健康研究提供开放技术资源。

Abstract: Penile measurement is clinically relevant across male reproductive and urogenital health, including conditions such as micropenis, congenital and endocrine disorders, and sexual or urinary dysfunction. However, quantitative assessment of penile size has relied mainly on external length or circumference measurements, which are difficult to standardize, sensitive to measurement conditions, and unable to capture the internal portion of the penis. MRI enables volumetric assessment of the whole penis in vivo, but automated segmentation has not previously been established at population scale. Automated whole-organ volumetry would enable high-throughput phenotyping for multi-omics and clinical studies of male reproductive disease.
  Here, we present a deep learning framework for whole-penis segmentation in multi-channel DIXON MRI. Using a newly curated expert-annotated training dataset ($n = 145$ subjects; $13,050$ annotated slices) and a double-annotated independent test benchmark ($n = 24$ subjects; $2,160$ double-annotated slices), we optimized a 3D nnU-Net architecture. The model achieved a 5-fold cross-validation Dice score of $0.90$ and performed at observer-level accuracy on the independent test set (Dice: $0.92$; Hausdorff distance: $3.58$).
  We deployed the model in $34,412$ UK Biobank participants, enabling automated quantification of total penile tissue, including both external and internal components. Longitudinal evaluation in 2,282 men demonstrated high inter-session reproducibility ($r = 0.87$). This framework establishes a reproducible and population-scalable method for MRI-based assessment of penile anatomy and provides an open technical resource for future studies in urological imaging and male reproductive health. The trained model weights will be publicly released.

</details>


### [168] [Self-Auditing Residual Drifting for Pathology-Preserving Accelerated Knee MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02428)
*Qing Lyu,Jianxu Wang,Mohammad Kawas,Ge Wang,Christopher T. Whitlow*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出SA-RDM-DM，一种基于生成性漂移扩散模型的自审计加速MRI重建方法，在保持亚秒级推理的同时实现高保真重建，并能生成误差图和风险评分用于质量自审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速MRI从欠采样k空间重建时，现有方法可能模糊诊断相关结构或引入全局指标无法捕获的失败模式，缺乏对重建质量的自动审计能力。

Method: 将生成性漂移范式适配到加速MRI，训练物理条件化的漂移场从零填充重建向全采样残差校正演化；联合预测图像域和缺失k空间残差校正，强制数据一致性，使用频率感知和残差漂移监督恢复细节，并在同一推理过程中生成密集误差图和切片级风险评分。

Result: 在fastMRI膝盖数据上（加速因子4/8/12），SSIM指标超越所有基线；亚秒级推理，避免迭代扩散方法的长采样时间；病理区域结构保真度更优；自审计评分能有效识别高误差重建。

Conclusion: 该方法联合考虑图像保真度、病理保存、运行时间和病例特异性可靠性，为加速MRI重建评估提供了更全面的范式。

Abstract: Accelerated magnetic resonance imaging reduces acquisition time, but reconstruction from undersampled k-space can blur diagnostically relevant structures or introduce failures that are not captured by global image metrics. We propose SA-RDM-DC, a Self-Auditing Residual generative Drifting Model with Data Consistency for accelerated knee MRI. The method adapts the newly proposed generative drifting paradigm to accelerated MRI by training a physics-conditioned drift field from the zero-filled reconstruction toward the fully sampled residual correction. It predicts image- and missing-k-space residual corrections, enforces data consistency with acquired k-space, uses frequency-aware and residual drifting supervision to recover fine detail, and produces dense error maps and slice-level risk scores in the same inference pass. We evaluate SA-RDM-DC on multi-coil fastMRI knee data at acceleration factors of 4, 8, and 12, with fastMRI+ pathology annotations for region-level and classifier-based task preservation, and on SKM-TEA for zero-shot and fine-tuned protocol-shift evaluation. Compared with zero-filled reconstruction, UNet-image-SENSE, DC-UNet, Score-Diffusion, ELF-Diff, SENSE-VarNet, and MoDL baselines, SA-RDM-DC achieves the highest SSIM across fastMRI acceleration factors while retaining subsecond per-slice inference and avoiding the long sampling time of iterative diffusion baselines. In pathology-aware analysis, SA-RDM-DC preserves lesion-region structural fidelity and reduces meniscus prediction instability. Its self-auditing scores strongly identify high-error reconstructions on fastMRI and partially transfer as a selective-review signal under SKM-TEA protocol shift. These results support reconstruction evaluation that jointly considers image fidelity, pathology preservation, runtime, and case-specific reliability.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个模块化神经符号框架，通过将预测与推理分离（神经网络+符号推理），在生成反事实解释时显式融入领域知识和干预约束，确保解释的可行性与可操作性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法缺乏显式机制来融入领域知识和干预约束，导致生成的解释往往不现实或不可行。

Method: 提出PACE框架，采用模块化神经符号架构：神经预测模型负责分类，符号推理层（使用回答集编程ASP）在反事实生成过程中强制执行领域特定约束，对可行干预进行显式建模。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束能够生成更符合领域可行性要求的解释，同时揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法在可解释AI中具有潜力，能够生成透明、可行感知的反事实解释；PACE框架是模型无关的，可适应需要现实决策支持的领域。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [170] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Auto-FL-Research(AFR)，一个用于联邦学习算法配方搜索的约束编码智能体工作流，在多个医疗和LEAF数据集上评估，发现部分改进来自FL机制变化，部分来自固定表面的调参效果，还有部分为单次运行伪像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究涉及大量细小的算法选择（优化器变体、服务器聚合规则、本地训练调度、归一化、正则化、模型架构等），这些选择手动探索成本高昂，且当候选改变同时影响训练或评估路径时难以公平比较。

Method: 提出AFR，一种约束编码智能体工作流。智能体可提出并实现候选训练算法（包括服务器聚合规则、客户端更新调度、本地目标和注册模型变体），任务配置固定变异表面、计算预算、通信合约和最终模型评估。每个campaign记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物和失败状态。

Result: 在5个跨中心医疗FLamby任务和6个LEAF数据集上评估，5次重复实验在4个FLamby任务和5个LEAF任务上取得改进。同时发现了对种子敏感和搜索选择的失败案例。对比控制实验显示，部分改进对应FL配方变化，其他改进可被固定表面标量控制复现，或在重复/保留评估下失败。

Conclusion: 混合结果本身就是贡献：展示了如何将智能体生成的候选分为三类——可重复的FL机制、固定表面调参效果、选中的单次运行伪像。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [171] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Wiola是一个从头构建、不继承任何现有模型家族架构的完全原创小语言模型（SLM），提出了五项独立创新的组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应令牌合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和WiolaRMSNorm，提供120M到1.5B四种规模，完全兼容HuggingFace生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型（如GPT、LLaMA、Mistral等）在架构上存在高度同质化，缺乏结构多样性探索。作者旨在从第一性原理出发，设计一套完全原创的SLM架构，通过多项创新组件来探索新的建模范式。

Method: Wiola架构包含五个核心创新模块：(1) SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置编码，融合绝对、相对和层级位置信号；(2) GCLA：每个解码器层可软性交叉注意前面两层的压缩摘要，增强层间一致性；(3) ATM：在中间网络层动态合并语义冗余的相邻token，降低注意力复杂度；(4) DSFF：用两个并行流经学习门控融合替代传统MLP；(5) WiolaRMSNorm：引入逐维可学习偏移向量防止表征坍塌。模型提供120M/360M/700M/1.5B四种参数规模。

Result: Wiola提供了完整的数学推导、架构框图、复杂度分析，并与GPT-2、LLaMA-2、Mistral进行了系统对比。所有22项架构单元测试通过，完全兼容HuggingFace Transformers生态。

Conclusion: Wiola证明了从零设计完全原创的SLM架构是可行的，五项创新组件各自独立有效，为语言模型架构多样性提供了新方向，且保持了与主流生态系统的兼容性。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [172] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agent4cs 是一个多智能体框架，通过自底向上的方式对大型代码库进行摘要生成。它包含三个专用智能体：摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证。在7个前沿模型上评估，相比结构化提示基线，语义一致性平均提升8%，关键词覆盖率提升高达38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解大型复杂代码库（尤其是结构混淆、文档不完整）仍然是一个重大挑战。现有代码摘要方案通常依赖单一语言模型，将源码视为纯文本，未能充分利用仓库内的丰富相互依赖和层次信息。

Method: 提出了 Agent4cs 多智能体框架，采用自底向上的摘要生成方式：(1) 摘要生成智能体负责生成稳健摘要；(2) 关键词提取智能体主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；(3) 质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上评估，与两种含代码片段的结构化提示基线相比，所有文件夹层级的语义一致性平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs 通过多智能体协作和自底向上的策略，有效改进了大型代码库的摘要质量，在语义一致性和关键词覆盖方面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [173] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话维持在低成本基线路径，将有操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流（包含冲突感知通信和写前重新考虑机制），在零售和航空任务中显著提升了可靠性，避免了统一施加额外控制的开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服代理从对话交互转向操作执行（退款、取消、改单等），这带来了服务控制问题：如何在保持常规服务快速低摩擦的同时，防止因客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入之间的交互导致的操作错误。

Method: 提出难度路由服务控制架构：(1) 轻量级路由器将常规会话保留在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合会话路由至升级工作流；(2) 升级路径使用冲突感知通信和写前重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中推理和安全检查。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法在存在操作冲突的服务请求上持续提升可靠性；路由证据表明更强的控制定向于冲突请求而非常规请求；对话和工具使用分析显示提升并非来自无差别扩展交互或工具链，而是新增轮次和工具调用用于证据收集、写分离和写前重新考虑。

Conclusion: 升级工作流能够保留备用计划、将检索记录绑定到正确操作、序列化写入并分解多实体请求。航空任务结果验证了相同的服务控制逻辑在预订操作中的可扩展性。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow. The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [174] [CreativityNeuro: Steering Language Model Weights to Improve Divergent Thinking and Reduce Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01433)
*Samuel Schapiro,Core Francisco Park,Felix Sosa,Lav R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CreativityNeuro是一种无需数据的对比权重引导方法，通过减少模式坍塌来增强大语言模型的发散性思维，在多项创造力评估中显著提升原创性、惊喜度和创造力，并在未见任务上展现出良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在开放式问题中倾向于生成相似的响应（人工蜂群效应），缺乏发散性思维能力，现有方法需要行为数据、重训练或梯度微调，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: 提出CreativityNeuro，一种基于对比权重引导（contrastive weight steering）的无数据方法，通过在权重空间而非激活空间进行干预来增强LLM的发散性思维。

Result: 在DAT任务上提升高达14个人类百分位点；在AUT和Task Task的大规模人类评估（N=720）中，原创性、惊喜度和创造力显著提升；在所有三项任务中模式坍塌均显著减少；权重空间引导比激活空间引导具有更好的跨任务泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreativityNeuro无需行为数据、重训练或梯度微调即可有效提升LLM的发散性思维并减少模式坍塌，为增强LLM在创造性领域的表现提供了一种简便有效的方法。

Abstract: Divergent thinking is a crucial aspect of creativity, yet large language models (LLMs) tend to consistently generate similar responses to open-ended questions, in what has been termed the artificial hivemind effect. Here, we introduce CreativityNeuro, a data-free method for enhancing divergent thinking in LLMs via contrastive weight steering. We evaluate our method across multiple creativity assessments and report several main findings. On the Divergent Association Task (DAT), a vocabulary-space creativity test, CreativityNeuro improves performance by up to 14 human percentile points. Next, in a large-scale human evaluation (N=720) on the Alternative Uses Test (AUT) and the Task Task, CreativityNeuro achieves significant improvements in originality, surprise, and creativity, transferring to longer-form and more open-ended tasks. Importantly, we find that across all three tasks, CreativityNeuro demonstrably reduces measures of mode collapse. Moreover, activation steering achieves comparable performance to CreativityNeuro on the DAT, but it does not transfer to the AUT and Task Task, demonstrating the effectiveness of weight-space steering in generalizing to unseen tasks. In conclusion, CreativityNeuro improves divergent thinking and reduces mode collapse without requiring behavioral data, re-training, or gradient-based fine-tuning, providing a straightforward way to enhance LLM performance in creative domains.

</details>


### [175] [Discrete Diffusion Language Models for Interactive Radiology Report Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01436)
*Max Van Puyvelde,Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Wim Van Criekinge,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次将扩散语言模型（DiffusionGemma-26B）应用于医疗视觉问答任务，证明其在性能上与同体量的自回归模型（Gemma-4-26B）持平甚至更优，同时解码速度提升3.5-4.4倍，并具备自回归模型所缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，更符合临床报告的实际写作模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗基础模型几乎全部采用自回归（从左到右）生成范式，而扩散语言模型在文生文任务上已展现出竞争力，但其在医疗领域的应用尚未被探索。作者希望验证扩散模型能否替代自回归模型，并利用其双向去噪特性解决医疗报告生成中的实际痛点。

Method: 对混合专家扩散语言模型DiffusionGemma-26B进行微调，使用与自回归模型Gemma-4-26B相同的LoRA策略，在医疗视觉问答数据集上进行对比评测，采用对冗长性鲁棒的LLM作为评分器。通过解码速度测量和任意顺序填充实验验证扩散模型的优势。

Result: 扩散模型在所有医疗视觉问答数据集上均匹配或超过自回归模型性能；微调后的模型（3.8B激活参数）与前沿视觉语言模型具有竞争力；解码速度比自回归快3.5-4.4倍；扩散模型天然支持任意顺序填充，而自回归模型在此任务上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 扩散语言模型在医疗领域不仅具备与自回归模型相当的性能和更快的解码速度，还提供了自回归模型缺乏的任意顺序填充能力，这更符合临床报告中医生片段式、非一致性的实际写作方式，具有重要的应用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion language models, which generate text by denoising a token canvas bidirectionally instead of emitting tokens left to right, have become competitive with autoregressive (AR) generation. Medical foundation models, however, remain almost entirely autoregressive. We adapt a mixture-of-experts diffusion language model, DiffusionGemma-26B, and benchmark it against its same-size AR sibling Gemma-4-26B under an identical LoRA recipe on medical visual question answering datasets, scored by a verbosity-robust LLM judge. Diffusion matches or exceeds AR on all of them, and the finetuned model (3.8B active) is competitive with frontier vision-language models; its decoding is also 3.5-4.4x faster. Beyond this parity, the diffusion model offers a drafting capability AR lacks: any-order infill. Because the canvas is denoised bidirectionally, a radiologist can fix report fragments and have the model fill the text between them, an operation inherent to diffusion but not to autoregression, which is subpar at it. This suits real reports, which are often terse or inconsistent across clinicians and institutions.

</details>


### [176] [Beyond Next-Token Prediction: An RLVR Proof of Concept for Tool-Use Agents on Atlassian Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01465)
*Karthikeya Aditya Vissa,Sankalp Mane,Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在目标环境中直接训练小模型，以解决LLM在调用企业级SaaS API时因目标不匹配（预测下一个token vs. 执行正确API调用）导致的静默失败问题。在五个高保真合成Jira/Confluence环境中，RL训练后的Qwen3.5-4B在非退化场景下将平均奖励从0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00，其中Confluence页面创建场景从0.35升至1.00。但该方法难以扩展到少量端点之外的场景，且部分场景基线已饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练目标（预测下一个token）与在企业级SaaS API中正确执行操作（调用正确端点、传递正确嵌套参数、遵循正确顺序）之间存在目标不匹配（objective mismatch），导致在实际工作流中出现静默失败（silent failures），如字段缺失、工具幻觉、过早停止等。

Method: 构建五个高保真合成环境（模拟Jira REST v3和Confluence v2 API模式），使用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）直接在目标环境中训练；奖励完全从工具调用轨迹计算，无需真实API、无需学习型评判器、无需人工标注；采用GRPO算法训练Qwen3-1.7B和Qwen3.5-4B模型。

Result: 在四个非退化场景中，RL训练策略将平均奖励从4B基线的0.35-0.92提升至0.95-1.00；最大单场景提升来自Confluence页面创建（0.35→1.00）。一个场景（ticket-transition）的奖励形状饱和，4B基线已满分。

Conclusion: 这是向面向小众企业API的、以结果优化为导向的小模型迈出的初步步骤。但存在两个主要局限：手工设计可验证奖励无法扩展到本文报告的少量端点之外，且部分场景奖励形状饱和导致基线已无提升空间。

Abstract: Large language models are trained to predict the next token, not to act inside a specific API. In niche enterprise SaaS workflows -- where success means hitting the right endpoint with the right nested arguments in the right order -- this objective mismatch shows up as silent failures: dropped required fields, hallucinated tools, or early stops after a single read. We ask whether Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), applied directly in the target environment, closes the gap. As a proof of concept we build a suite of five synthetic environments emulating the Jira REST v3 and Confluence v2 APIs at schema fidelity; rewards are computed entirely from the tool-call trace, with no live API, no learned judge, and no human label in the loop. Scoring prompted Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3.5-4B on the same checkers that drive GRPO training, we find that on the four scenarios whose rewards are non-degenerate the RL-trained policy lifts average reward from a 4B-baseline range of 0.35--0.92 to 0.95--1.00, with the largest single gain on Confluence page creation ($0.35 \rightarrow 1.00$). We position this as a preliminary step toward outcome-optimised small models for niche enterprise APIs, and foreground two limitations a workshop reader should weigh: hand-crafting verifiable rewards does not scale beyond the handful of endpoints reported here, and one of our five scenarios (ticket-transition) has a saturating reward shape that the prompted 4B already maxes out.

</details>


### [177] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文审计了MedAgentBench v1/v2，发现41.7%的静默完成上限使不作为成为RL主导策略，构建了MAB-v3（8.9%上限）。训练Qwen3-8B揭示了能力上限（10/20任务类型零基础性能）和格式-知识障碍（3/20类型需要精确临床代码）两个结构性障碍。纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，规则SFT为34.1%，差距完全归因于这些障碍。提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法可预测RL可学习性，并给出解决方案：SFT注入代码，RL学习条件逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（检查化验值、应用阈值、生成结构化FHIR医嘱）天然适合基于世界反馈的RL，因为临床专家将决策逻辑编码为验证器后，该验证器可对无限 rollout 进行评分而无需逐条标注。但应用RL需要合理的反馈通道和足够的基础能力，现有基准存在缺陷。

Method: 审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现静默完成问题；构建MAB-v3（508个任务，8.9%上限）；使用Qwen3-8B进行纯RL训练与规则SFT对比实验；提出决策/格式知识/查找分类法分析RL可学习性。

Result: 纯RL达到18.2% pass@1，规则SFT达到34.1%，15.9个百分点的差距完全由能力上限和格式-知识障碍导致。

Conclusion: RL在临床协议执行任务上存在结构性学习障碍；能力上限（零梯度）和格式-知识障碍（代码不可发现）限制了RL的有效性；建议使用SFT注入知识代码，用RL学习条件决策逻辑。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling).
